- **impeccable-adapt**: Recontextualize an existing web design for a different screen size, device, platform, or use case. Use when adapting rather than extending a design - the trap is treating adaptation as scaling, when the real job is rethinking the experience for the new context. Covers assessing what breaks, phone-to-tablet restructuring, orientation and foldables, platform-to-platform idiom translation, and verifying custom controls under real touch input. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-adapt) · ★4)

- **impeccable-adapt**: Impeccable adapt UI quality skill. Use when auditing or improving web interface implementation with the adapt workflow. ([KAYANSTOR/AI](https://github.com/KAYANSTOR/AI/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/03-UI-UX-Design/impeccable/adapt) · 3 copies)
- **impeccable-adapt-native**: Recontextualize an existing native design (iOS / Android / adaptive) for a different device class, orientation, platform, or origin. Use when adapting a native app, where the trap is treating adaptation as scaling. Covers size classes, window size classes, posture and hinge handling on foldables, and translating idioms rather than transplanting them between iOS and Android. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-adapt-native) · ★4)
- **impeccable-android**: Android platform conventions for native apps built with Jetpack Compose, Android Views, React Native, Expo, or Flutter. Use when building or reviewing Android UI. Covers Material Design 3 as the governing rulebook, expressing brand through Material theming, the Android slop test for off-spec components, and the OS guarantees iOS hardware still owes. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-android) · ★4)
- **impeccable-animate**: Review a feature and enhance it with purposeful animations, micro-interactions, and motion effects that improve usability and delight. ([LineLuLan/line-harness](https://github.com/LineLuLan/line-harness/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/impeccable-animate) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **impeccable-animate**: Add purposeful motion to a user interface.
